([guyvinograd/claude-plugins](https://github.com/guyvinograd/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/say-plugin/skills/say))
- **say**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "say" something out loud, e.g. "say hello", "say all done", "say the task is complete". Speaks the message through the system speakers using Windows TTS. ([guyvinograd/say-plugin](https://github.com/guyvinograd/say-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/say))
- **Say it plainly**: Use when a draft sounds like an AI wrote it, or someone asks to make it less AI, more human, plain English, or like them. Rewrites it, facts unchanged. ([adjustleads/provenskills-free-packs](https://github.com/adjustleads/provenskills-free-packs/tree/HEAD/plain-writing/say-it-plainly))
- **say-ducklake-xor**: Parallel thread/DuckLake discovery with XOR uniqueness from gay_seed. Finds "say" or MCP usage, cross-refs with all DuckDB sources, launches bounded parallel ops. ([plurigrid/asi](https://github.com/plurigrid/asi/tree/HEAD/skills/say-ducklake-xor) · ★64 · 2 copies)
